### jZ8tIoUDPlY — My Biggest Loss in More Than 4 Years... (43:38, ~2025; date said "October 7th")
1. Rules / statements
   1. COVERED — leading gapper / obvious stock chosen at 06:53-07:00 scan; sits down 07:00; focus window 07:00-09:30/10:00 "get green and get out".
   2. COVERED — five pillars as core of strategy; no-news biotech (GLTO) was a "B quality" setup, he says taking B setups in a hot market was NOT the mistake.
   3. NEW (17:01) — Position-size ladder: "quarter size until green one quarter of goal". Daily goal $20,000 -> trade ~10,000 shares until up $5,000 (a quarter of goal). Breach: took ~10,000 sh on a $30 stock first trade (-$20k, ~-$2/sh), then larger on trade 2 (50-90k sh) and 40,000 sh on trade 4.
   4. NEW (18:33-19:03) — Escalation rule (what NOT to do): increasing share size after a loss/while red = classic spiral. Rule is size DOWN when red. (Partly COVERED by "size up only after a cushion"; the explicit "reduce when red" + quarter-size-until-quarter-goal is the new numeric form.)
   5. NEW (14:30) — Re-entry after a violent rejection: "Once a stock does that once (a ~$3/share knife / false breakout at a level), it'll do it again." First trade lost ~$20k on a rejection at ~$30; second entry on the break of 32-35 knifed again from 34.75 to ~25 in one candle. Implied rule: after a >=~$3 (~10%) one/two-bar rejection at a level, do not re-enter breakouts of that level the same day.
   6. NEW (22:00, 44:xx n/a) — Price: lower-priced stocks better for him; $4->$8 squeeze made +$23k clean; both big losses (Feb 4 2021 and today) were on ~$30 stocks. (Price pillar $2-$20 = COVERED; he adds it as evidence only.)
   7. NEW (9:01) — Failed VWAP-break entry: bought VWAP break (CISS), popped, then rejected on a high-volume red candle; he labels it a bad trade (+$2k only). No quantified rule.
   8. COVERED — first 1-min candle to make a new high after a pullback on a squeeze = "take every day of the week" (+$23k, ULY) [22:00].
   9. COVERED — max daily loss: he was far below max loss and "shouldn't take any more trades"; took one anyway (principle).
   10. COVERED — cut loss fast, "rip the band-aid off" (he sold the knife at -$5/sh in-candle).
   11. NEW (12:30-13:30) — Failure to bank: was +$35k unrealized (~+$3/sh, green on day by $10k) on 15k sh, did not sell, ADDED at the break of 35 to 40,000 sh (avg ~$34), then it reversed to -$3 to -$5/sh = -$188k on the stock. Rule implied: never add into a big open winner at a round-number breakout; take half into strength (the half-off exit is COVERED; the "do not add at resistance after a spike" is new).
   12. NEW (30:32-31:02) — Post-drawdown throttle ("trader rehab"): after a big loss day, treat the loss as high-water mark; trade reduced size until half of the loss (~$85k of $172k) has been earned back; then size normal. Examples of triggers: 2-4 consecutive big red days, or a single loss so large he cannot trust himself. Also broker-enforced hard limits: max share size and max red-day P&L; account locks if breached.
   13. NEW (36:30-37:30) — Recovery targets: "base hits" $3-5k each, 3-4 per day = ~$15k/day, closed by ~09:30-10:00; do not overstay.
   14. NEW (2:31) — Market regime: in a hot market (whole year) he drifts to the periphery of the strategy (B setups, bigger risk) and is "corrected" when the market cools. Discretionary.
   15. NOT taking more trades once he judged day compromised; moved to the small account (separate-account attention split is a self-noted issue).
2. Statistics (all self-reported)
   - Day: -$172k net; GLTO round trip(s) -$188k on one stock (first entry ~10,000 sh at $29.27, -$20k; second 15k sh @32.35 -> 40k sh avg ~$34; one 1-min candle fell ~$9-10; ~-$195k at worst).
   - Other trades: CISS +$2k; ZNB -$13k main (-$53 small), later ZNB +$5k; ULY +$23k; small account net green (ZNB +$166.48).
   - 2021-02-04: -$274-275k on a $30 stock; recovered in <2 weeks; month finished +$300k.
   - YTD 3,033 trades. Last 90 days ~+$4M; since Aug 1 ~+$1.1M before today (~$950k net). A $100k green day a week earlier; two prior red days -$58k and -$45k back to back.
   - Claims >90% chance of recovering the loss in ~3 weeks if he follows rules.
3. Codeable?
   - #3/#4 size ladder: codeable from account P&L + bars (state machine: qty cap = 25% until day P&L >= 25% of daily goal). Needs a daily-goal parameter.
   - #5 re-entry block after violent rejection: codeable from 1-min bars (detect >=X% drop within N bars from a breakout high; block that symbol's re-entry same day).
   - #11 no-add-at-resistance: codeable from 1-min bars + position state.
   - #12 post-drawdown throttle: codeable from daily P&L history (half-loss recovery rule).
   - #7, #13, #14: discretionary / not precisely specified.
4. Most useful NEW: same-day re-entry block after a violent (~$3 / ~10%) rejection at a level, plus size ladder (25% size until day P&L reaches 25% of goal).
5. Portion read: 100% (88/88 30-s blocks, 0:01-43:32).

### yFoBnM0iSlc — How to use the Relative Volume Trading Strategy (with ZERO experience) (39:34, ~2020; refs to airlines/Buffett selling, 3.5-4 yrs of data)
1. Rules
   1. COVERED — RVOL >=5x average (platform column shows 50-day; he is unsure 14/30/50) as core stock-selection filter; RVOL of ~3 "not high enough" (NEO 3.34 rejected despite 140M total volume) [6:00-7:30].
   2. COVERED — trade leading % gappers (1st-3rd) = the "obvious" stocks; trade with the herd [16:30-17:30].
   3. COVERED — needs a catalyst (can be sector/global headline, e.g. "Chinese schools closing"); no-news former momentum stock = less predictable [14:32-15:30, 26:00].
   4. COVERED — first-candle-new-high, VWAP respected; 68-70% accuracy; 1:1 avg win/loss.
   5. NEW (8:32, 38:31) — Pre-market RVOL proxy (software shows no RVOL pre-market): eyeball pre-market volume vs PRIOR DAY total volume. Example: 12M shares by 9:15-9:20 vs 1.3M prior day = "already RVOL ~10". Codeable: premarket cumulative volume / prior-day volume.
   6. NEW (29:30-31:30) — Continuation: much of profit is on day-2 stocks whose RVOL vs 50-day is still >=5 even if day RVOL vs prior day is <1 (e.g. 2x or lower than day 1). So the filter is RVOL vs ~30/50-day average, not vs yesterday. Codeable from daily bars.
   7. NEW (15:31, 33:00) — Low pre-market total volume does not by itself exclude: stock up 24% on 19,000 sh pre-market considered if it is likely to reach high RVOL (judged from gap %, headline, daily chart, prior-day volume). Discretionary.
   8. NEW (13:30, 27:00) — Daily-chart check: "no upside resistance above this level" (WIMI ~$6; prior highs overhead). Also obvious intraday levels used as targets: e.g. break of HOD 8.45 -> target 9.25; PM high 8.50 [27:00]. Codeable from daily/1-min bars (distance to prior swing high / PM high / HOD).
   9. NEW (18:00-19:30) — Claim: pattern reliability (first candle new high, VWAP support/rejection, topping-tail doji) depends on high RVOL ("only works because people respect it"); low-RVOL stocks give unpredictable resolution (called "off in the weeds"; mostly HFT/institutional flow). Testable: split MCL/MC5 trades by RVOL bucket.
   10. NEW (34:00) — Entry type: red-to-green move (stock red on day, curls up through prior close/flips green), WAFU ~$7.80 -> $9.40. Codeable from daily + 1-min (cross of prior close).
   11. NEW (35:00-36:31) — Quick-profit vs hold trade-off, stated: scalping +20c/sh gives high accuracy; for each $1 runner "5 other times it goes up then returns to breakeven". ### 1FKu4LH0Xss — How to AVOID False Breakouts (Use This Technical Indicator) (35:41, ~2025; refs 2022 bear market, VST and CXAI "yesterday")
1. Rules
   1. COVERED — MACD (1-min) must be open/positive; MACD against the trade = no trade; MACD crossover marks end of front side [4:30, 13:00, 20:32].
   2. COVERED — micro pullback strategy: buy first pullback on front side; light-volume red candle on pullback ok; volume profile positive (heavier green) else skip [10:30-11:00, 31:31-32:01].
   3. COVERED — algo spike/flush mechanism; HFT pulls orders at break from consolidation [23:30-28:00]; early (pre-market 06:00-08:00) is cleanest, chop starts at the open [8:30-9:00]; buy dips after flush [6:02].
   4. COVERED — avoid buying extended off the 9 EMA (5-min extension) [14:30-15:00].
   5. NEW (15:30-16:01) — False-breakout risk checklist (5 flags): (1) MACD against trade; (2) volume profile = more volume on red candles; (3) stock already has a history of false breakouts/high-volume rejection candles that day; (4) several topping tails in a row, or topping tails at the resistance level; (5) consolidating (sideways) too long. More flags = skip the breakout. Flags 2-5 are codeable composites; flag 1 COVERED.
   6. NEW (0:00-2:30) — Repeated taps at resistance: 5 tests of $7.00 failed (4 taps then break); breakout candle then went to $7.60 and dumped to $6.00 on 1.5M shares in one candle. His view: many failed taps = "dam" that often breaks but the break was a bull trap. Codeable: count touches of a level (high within ~1% of a round/half-dollar level) over N minutes.
   7. NEW (4:01) — On the first pullback, if price cannot hold the 9 EMA (1-min), "the easy part is over" -> stop trading that leg. If pullback holds 9 EMA and goes higher, keep trading. Codeable.
   8. NEW (33:31-34:00) — Second leg after a deep pullback (to the 9 MA on 5-min following a parabolic first leg) does worse: crowded, thick, big sellers. Trade front side only. Codeable: leg counter / distance to 5-min 9 EMA.
   9. NEW (14:00-15:00) — Cross-reference the 5-min chart: 3-4 big green 5-min candles then a pullback = flag/ABCD; if 5-min is extended off the 9 MA and 1-min MACD is negative, odds of a flush are high. Codeable.
   10. NEW (12:00-12:31) — Stock-profile example: float 1.3M, already up >=50% when first seen, price $5-$10 = "sweet spot" (lower price at start gave him pause). RVOL high + news. Pillars COVERED; the $5-$10 sweet spot and ">=50% already up" are NEW tightenings. Codeable (price band) / needs float data.
   11. NEW (30:31-31:00) — Daily-chart checklist after scanner hit: nearby overhead resistance, 200-day MA location, gaps/"windows"; extra edge if recent IPO or recent reverse split. Daily bars codeable (200 MA, gap fills, overhead highs); IPO/reverse split needs corporate-action data.
   12. NEW (6:02-6:32, 13:00) — Dip-buy stop = low of the pullback/flush; wait for red to turn green and for buying to show on time & sales, then buy. Stop placement: LOW OF PULLBACK. Time & sales part needs tape; stop rule codeable from bars.
   13. NEW (20:32-21:00) — MACD-negative veto applies mainly to BREAKOUT buys and VWAP dips; buying a deep flush while MACD negative "you're already getting in on a low" so he tolerates it. Also he sometimes takes MACD-negative trades "higher risk, smaller size" [34:31-35:00]. Codeable.
   14. NEW (29:01-29:30) — If a single 50k-100k share order prints within a minute early in a move = someone is unloading; stop trading that stock (many false breakouts follow). Needs tape/level 2 (large-print detection from 1-min volume spike is a proxy).

### mBT72-bFlPM — Trading was HARD Until I Learned this BASE HIT Strategy (29:17, 2017 story told in a later year, "2024" referenced)
1. Rules
   1. NEW (13:00-15:30): reduce share size; focus on "base hits" (winners $0-$4,000, losers $0-$2,000); smaller size = less slippage and easier cutting of losses — NEW (position size cap; his data: avg 15,000 shares in oversized trades vs 6,000-10,000 in profitable zone)

1. Rules
   1. NEW (06:30-08:00): Market regime cycle in 4 stages — cold (low volatility, news stocks do not react, buyers scared, nobody makes money) -> spark (one surprise stock moves huge and does not fail, shorts squeezed) -> hot/sustained (further stocks matching the spark's profile hold up, more buying less selling, 200-300% moves) -> exhausted (copycat weak-headline companies pop and fade, company/insider selling, huge reversals and rejections) -> cold again — NEW (qualitative; no thresholds)

### V9za4wt9ONo — How to Hold Winners Longer (21:16, year not stated; audit quoted "start 1 Jan 2017", "over 10 million" gross)
1. Rules
   1. NEW (15:00-17:30): scale-out ladder — sell HALF the position at first +10c (on 2,000 shares risking 10c = $200, +10c pays $100), move stop to BREAKEVEN on the rest (risk on remainder = zero); then sell half of what remains at +25c / +30c / +40c; keep halving (1,000 -> 500 -> 250); "initial position covers the risk, scale-outs can make more than the initial position" — NEW (breakeven stop after first partial and repeated halving ladder; COVERED only has "half off into strength, rest on first red 1-min close")
   2. NEW (17:30-19:00): daily-goal staging — until daily goal is hit be cautious, take only highest-quality trades (usually 3-4 trades to reach goal); after goal is hit, per-trade risk = amount of cushion above goal ($100 over goal -> risk $100; $300 over -> risk $300); winners then allowed to roll more instead of scaling out immediately — NEW (numeric cushion-based risk budget; COVERED only has "size up only after a cushion")
   3. NEW (11:00-12:00): trade frequency cut from 15-20 trades/day to ~1-2 per day (quality over quantity) and accuracy rose — NEW (trade-count cap; he says first couple trades only goal is to get green)
   4. NEW (06:30-08:30): overtrading every name on the high-of-day scanner gave ~50-55% accuracy per 100 trades and slippage turned 5c stops into 15-30c losses — NEW (stat; supports gate on number of trades/quality)
   5. NEW (10:30-11:30, 13:30): trade the front side of the move, not the back side / avoid false breakouts — NEW (qualitative; extension of fresher-name rule)
   6. NEW (11:30-13:00): cut losers immediately; best trades work immediately, worst fail immediately, sideways = bail; exit by Ctrl-Z then hit the bid (not market order — HFT/market makers cause slippage on market orders) — NEW for the "limit at the bid, not market order" exit mechanic; breakout-or-bailout itself COVERED
